     │ is a change to one                                                      │
     │ function rather than to every call site. That seam is already built and │
     │ tested. Say so in the                                                   │
     │ README's "what is not built" section rather than leaving it as an       │
     │ unexplained gap.                                                        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ A10. Tests and evaluation                                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Vitest, targeting roughly 90 to 120 real tests. Not coverage theatre,   │
     │ these specific things:                                                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - money.test.ts — Indian grouping, GST split summing exactly to total,  │
     │ no float leakage.                                                       │
     │ - posting.test.ts — every event type debits equal credits; the          │
     │ invariant holds across the                                              │
     │ entire generated dataset.                                               │
     │ - signals.test.ts — each of the four signal functions, fixtured pass    │
     │ and fail.                                                               │
     │ - match.test.ts — a fixture per injected defect class, asserting the    │
     │ decision the engine should                                              │
     │ reach. The split-payment fixture asserts it fails, and asserts it       │
     │ produces a                                                              │
     │ SPLIT_PAYMENT_SUSPECTED exception rather than a wrong match.            │
     │ - assign.test.ts — no external record is ever claimed twice.            │
     │ - khata.test.ts — FIFO settlement, aging bucket boundaries at exactly   │
     │ 7, 15 and 30 days.                                                      │
     │ - forecast.test.ts — a synthetic weekly restock pattern is detected     │
     │ with the right cadence.                                                 │
     │ - tax.test.ts — a ₹50 GST mismatch is caught, a ₹0.50 rounding          │
     │ difference is not.                                                      │
     │ - agent/tools.test.ts — every tool handler against a fixed store,       │
     │ asserting spokenSummary                                                 │
     │ contains the engine's number and the handler rejects malformed args.    │
     │ - agent/numerals.test.ts — dhai, sava, paune, derh, bara sau, lakh,     │
     │ crore and                                                               │
     │ their Devanagari forms resolve to the right quantity, and an            │
     │ unrecognised token is rejected                                          │
     │ rather than guessed.                                                    │
     │ - limits/budget.test.ts — with a fake clock: the rolling window         │
     │ ceilings at 70% of TPM, budget                                          │
     │ exhaustion selects the exact next rung of the ladder, the 8-minute cap  │
     │ closes the session, the                                                 │
     │ resume carries deterministic ledger state, and the Firestore limiter    │
     │ refuses the 31st write.                                                 │
     │ - gemini/client.test.ts — the model fallback chain, mocked at the SDK   │
     │ boundary (port                                                          │
     │ Adhikaar's version of this test).                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ eval/run.ts — runs the full reconciliation against the ground truth and │
     │ prints a table:                                                         │
     │ records processed, matched, review, exceptions, precision, recall, F1,  │
     │ mean confidence on                                                      │
     │ correct matches vs incorrect ones, and runtime. This output is what the │
     │ README's evaluation table                                               │
     │ quotes, and it is the honest version of the track's "measured accuracy" │
     │ requirement.                                                            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ PART B — Deployment                                                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ All CLI. gh is already authenticated as harshkawatra11. vercel@54.13.0  │
     │ and gcloud are                                                          │
     │ installed. Firebase tooling runs via npx firebase-tools.                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 1. git init, .gitignore covering .env*.local, .data/, .next/,           │
     │ service-account JSON.                                                   │
     │ 2. Confirm identity before the first commit:                            │
     │ git config user.name "Harsh Kawatra" and git config user.email          │
     │ "harshkawatra11@gmail.com".                                             │
     │ 3. gh repo create harshkawatra11/hisaab --public --source=.             │
     │ --remote=origin --push.                                                 │
     │ Public, so a judge can open it.                                         │
     │ 4. GCP and Firestore:                                                   │
     │ gcloud projects create hisaab-app --name="Hisaab"                       │
     │ gcloud config set project hisaab-app                                    │
     │ gcloud services enable firestore.googleapis.com                         │
     │ gcloud firestore databases create --location=asia-south1                │
     │ gcloud iam service-accounts create hisaab-store                         │
     │ gcloud projects add-iam-policy-binding hisaab-app \                     │
     │   --member="serviceAccount:hisaab-store@hisaab-app.iam.gserviceaccount. │
     │ com" \                                                                  │
     │   --role="roles/datastore.user"                                         │
     │ gcloud iam service-accounts keys create ./sa.json \                     │
     │   --iam-account=hisaab-store@hisaab-app.iam.gserviceaccount.com         │
     │ 4. Scope the service account to roles/datastore.user only, exactly as   │
     │ Adhikaar does, then                                                     │
     │ delete sa.json from disk once the values are in Vercel. Set a GCP       │
     │ billing budget alert at                                                 │
     │ ₹1 as the second safety layer.                                          │
     │ 5. vercel link, then vercel env add for GEMINI_API_KEY,                 │
     │ FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID,                                                    │
     │ FIREBASE_CLIENT_EMAIL, FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY, SESSION_SECRET, across     │
     │ production,                                                             │
     │ preview and development.                                                │
     │ 6. .github/workflows/ci.yml: lint, then typecheck, then test, then next │
     │ build, cheapest                                                         │
     │ first. Do not add a standalone tsc --noEmit step ahead of the build if  │
     │ it proves flaky                                                         │
     │ on a clean checkout, Adhikaar hit exactly that failure because Next     │
     │ generates ambient route                                                 │
     │ types as a build side effect.                                           │
     │ 7. vercel --prod. Verify the live URL, then verify Firestore            │
     │ persistence across serverless                                           │
     │ instances with an independent curl against a different instance, the    │
     │ way the Adhikaar README                                                 │
     │ documents. That verification is a README claim, so it has to actually   │
     │ be run.                                                                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Commit discipline: small, real commits with plain messages, no em       │
     │ dashes, and the Co-Authored-By                                          │
     │ trailer. Never force-push main.                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ PART C — The README                                                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Model it directly on adhikaar/README.md, which is attached to this task │
     │ as the reference. Same                                                  │
     │ sections, same order, Hisaab's content:                                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 1. Centered header: name, one-line positioning, shields for live app,   │
     │ tests, license, Next.js,                                                │
     │ TypeScript, Firestore, Gemini, then an anchor nav row.                  │
     │ 2. "The claim this is built on" — the sourced problem statement. The    │
     │ MSME credit gap of                                                      │
     │ ₹20 to 25 lakh crore (U.K. Sinha RBI Expert Committee, June 2019); PM   │
     │ SVANidhi's 1.12 crore                                                   │
     │ loans worth ₹17,800 crore to 75.5 lakh street vendors (MoHUA); 89%      │
     │ account ownership with 16%                                              │
     │ of accounts inactive (Global Findex 2025). Then the honest caveat, the  │
     │ way Adhikaar caveats                                                    │
     │ its own register: these establish the size of the informal-credit       │
     │ economy, they are not a                                                 │
     │ measurement of reconciliation error rates, and no public                │
     │ transaction-level Indian vendor                                         │
     │ dataset exists, which is why the data here is synthetic and the         │
     │ generator is documented.                                                │
     │ 3. "One loop, not four separate tools" — the four track directions as   │
     │ consequences of one                                                     │
     │ ledger. Make the point structurally, not just in prose: the app is one  │
     │ master dashboard plus                                                   │
     │ three working surfaces, because reconciliation, settlement Q&A,         │
     │ forecasting and tax are four                                            │
     │ views of one ledger rather than four products bolted together. A        │
     │ screenshot of the master                                                │
     │ dashboard carries this argument faster than a paragraph does.           │
     │ 4. Mermaid mindmap of the actual stack, grouped by what each library    │
     │ does in this codebase.                                                  │
     │ 5. Technology cards table — every row tied to a real file path.         │
     │ 6. Mermaid architecture graph — voice and model layers drawn as a       │
     │ clearly separate,                                                       │
     │ optional, dashed side branch feeding only understanding and             │
     │ explanation, never computation.                                         │
     │ This mirrors Adhikaar's anti-wrapper doctrine drawing and is the visual │
     │ thesis of the repo.                                                     │
     │ 7. Mermaid sequence diagram — the reconciliation moneyshot: spoken      │
     │ event, structured event,                                                │
     │ posting, bank record arrives, three-layer match, exception raised,      │
     │ agent explains.                                                         │
     │ 8. Testing and evaluation — the real test count and the real            │
     │ precision/recall/F1 table from                                          │
     │ npm run eval. Publish the failures too, including the split-payment     │
     │ class, exactly as                                                       │
     │ Adhikaar publishes its 56.4% classifier number. A system that only      │
     │ reports its successes is                                                │
     │ not trustworthy, and this one is asked to make money-adjacent           │
     │ decisions.                                                              │
     │ 9. Running locally, zero-setup claim, then the optional Gemini and      │
     │ Firestore sections.                                                     │
     │ 10. Deployment, CI/CD (both halves verified against a real failure, not │
     │ assumed).                                                               │
     │ 11. "What this system decides, what it merely proposes" table. The rule │
     │ stated plainly:                                                         │
     │ the model may listen, structure and explain. It may never compute or    │
     │ post. Add a short                                                       │
     │ subsection on the token-budget limiter and the three-rung degradation   │
     │ ladder, with the real                                                   │
     │ TPM ceilings and the reasoning that session length, not session count,  │
     │ is what burns a Live                                                    │
     │ API quota. That paragraph is the kind of detail that reads as           │
     │ operational maturity.                                                   │
     │ 12. What is covered and what is not: the simulation boundary. No live   │
     │ UPI rails, no NPCI                                                      │
     │ membership, no Account Aggregator, no bank core write access. Synthetic │
     │ data, documented                                                        │
     │ generator. Not an accounting system of record and not tax advice. State │
     │ the deferred                                                            │
     │ login plainly here rather than leaving a judge to discover it: the      │
     │ prototype runs as a                                                     │
     │ single demo merchant, and the reason it costs nothing to add real auth  │
     │ later is that the                                                       │
     │ store layer already takes and checks ownerUid on every read and write,  │
     │ so authentication                                                       │
     │ slots into one function instead of every call site. WhatsApp settlement │
     │ reminders are named                                                     │
     │ as Phase 2 and the button in the Khata screen says so on its face.      │
     │ 13. Apache 2.0.                                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Same writing rules as everything else: no em dashes, no hooks, no slop. │
     │                                                                         │

     │                                                                         │
     │ D3. Slide-by-slide specification (9 slides)                             │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Ribbon carries seven tags, one per scored slide: Exec Summary · Problem │
     │ · Solution · How It Works · Tech Stack · Feasibility · Impact. Cover    │
     │ and Thank You carry no ribbon. Every                                    │
     │ content slide carries a bottom-right source line at 14pt. Every         │
     │ headline is an action title                                             │
     │ with a number, never a topic label.                                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The contrarian thesis, which the whole deck argues, decided per the     │
     │ skill's pattern A:                                                      │
     │ the obvious read of Track 04 is to automate an enterprise finance       │
     │ team's reconciliation. Hisaab                                           │
     │ argues the opposite. The highest-value finance-ops loop in India to     │
     │ close is not the one where                                              │
     │ both sides of the ledger already exist in a system. It is the one where │
     │ one side was never                                                      │
     │ written down at all, because it lives in a shopkeeper's head and in a   │
     │ paper bahi-khata. There                                                 │
     │ is nothing to reconcile until you create the missing side, and voice is │
     │ the only input method                                                   │
     │ that a shopkeeper serving a customer will actually use. The second      │
     │ beat: everyone will make the                                            │
     │ LLM the engine. Hisaab caps the model's authority in code so it can     │
     │ never post a match, because                                             │
     │ the stated 2026 bottleneck is verification capacity, and a model that   │
     │ grades its own work adds                                                │
     │ none.                                                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ Slide 1 — Cover. Archetype: full-bleed cover with layered card.         │
     │ Title Hisaab at 100 to 112pt. Sub-line: "The books, spoken into         │
     │ existence, then checked                                                 │
     │ against the bank." Chips: HackerVilla Nainital 2026, Track 04 · AI      │
     │ Finance Controller.                                                     │
     │ Team block: Harsh · Gursimran. No ribbon. One accent back-card behind   │
     │ the title block, ink                                                    │
     │ outline. Cover stays intentionally less dense than the content slides — │
     │ archetype A explicitly                                                  │
     │ calls for ample white space here, the density doctrine applies from     │
     │ slide 2 onward.                                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Slide 2 — Executive Summary. NEW. Archetype: H, executive summary       │
     │ (2-column for/against),                                                 │
     │ directly adapted from the skill's corpus pattern — the highest-leverage │
     │ slide in a winning deck,                                                │
     │ and the one the old 8-slide spec was missing as a standalone.           │
     │ Headline: the recommendation in one sentence, e.g. "Hisaab captures the │
     │ missing side of the                                                     │
     │ ledger first, then reconciles it, and caps the model below the          │
     │ auto-post line."                                                        │
     │ Sub-headline: the case question restated, e.g. "Track 04 asks for a     │
     │ finance-ops loop closed                                                 │
     │ across a batch of records. The obvious read is to build a better        │
     │ matcher."                                                               │
     │ - Two columns, opposite verdicts, symmetric structure (same bullet      │
     │ count, same weight):                                                    │
     │   - Left, THE OBVIOUS READ — automate enterprise-style reconciliation.  │
     │ Three bullets, each a                                                   │
     │ finding not a topic: matching engines assume both ledger sides already  │
     │ exist in a system;                                                      │
     │ the informal-credit economy's ₹20 to 25 lakh crore gap (U.K. Sinha      │
     │ Committee, RBI 2019) is                                                 │
     │ largely unrecorded transactions, not unmatched ones; a model that       │
     │ grades its own match adds                                               │
     │ no verification capacity, the stated 2026 bottleneck.                   │
     │   - Right, HISAAB'S MOVE — capture first, then reconcile, with capped   │
     │ model authority. Three                                                  │
     │ bullets: voice is the only input method a shopkeeper serving a customer │
     │ will actually use;                                                      │
     │ 75.5 lakh PM SVANidhi vendors already show the scale of credit no       │
     │ system has seen (MoHUA);                                                │
     │ the model's confidence is clamped at 0.89 in code, strictly below the   │
     │ 0.90 auto-post line, so                                                 │
     │ it can never post a match on its own.                                   │
     │ - All six bullets reuse figures already sourced elsewhere in the deck   │
     │ (§5 content-integrity rule:                                             │
     │ never introduce a number here that isn't backed on the slide where it's │
     │ detailed later).                                                        │
     │ - Source line: U.K. Sinha Committee, RBI 2019 | MoHUA.                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Slide 3 — Problem. Archetype: KPI hero band over a two-panel band with  │
     │ rails, closing on a                                                     │
     │ real MECE issue-tree diagram (upgraded from a flat strip in the old     │
     │ spec — a labelled tree with                                             │
     │ branch lines reads as a genuine decomposition, not a caption).          │
     │ Headline: "75.5 lakh vendors run on credit that no system has ever      │
     │ seen."                                                                  │
     │ - KPI hero row, three layered cards: ₹20 to 25 lakh crore (MSME credit  │
     │ gap), ₹17,800 crore                                                     │
     │ (PM SVANidhi disbursed), 89% / 16% (accounts owned vs inactive).        │
     │ Numerals 56 to 72pt,                                                    │
     │ alternating primary, primaryDeep, alert.                                │
     │ - Left rail panel, WHY: the loop never closes — the four-step failure:  │
     │ the sale on udhaar is                                                   │
     │ never recorded, the bank credit arrives days later under an             │
     │ unrecognisable narration, nobody                                        │
     │ reconciles the two, so the shopkeeper's cash position is a guess.       │
     │ - Right rail panel, WHO IT HITS — five actors, one line each: retailer, │
     │ wholesaler,                                                             │
     │ moneylender, D2C seller, B2B supplier. This is the user's explicit ask  │
     │ that the deck solve for                                                 │
     │ everyone in the chain, not only the kirana.                             │
     │ - Bottom band: a real decision-tree diagram (root THE BOOKS ARE WRONG,  │
     │ addChip nodes,                                                          │
     │ addDiagonalWire branch lines per the catalogue's Decision/strategy tree │
     │ recipe) branching into                                                  │
     │ three MECE causes: missing capture, unmatched settlement, untracked     │

     │ Global Findex 2025.                                                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Slide 4 — Solution. Archetype: hub-and-spoke with a contrarian callout  │
     │ band. This is the                                                       │
     │ slide that broke last time (the Voice-capture card overlapped and       │

     │ Headline: "One ledger, four loops: capture the missing side first, then │

     │ Slide 5 — How It Works. Archetype: punch-hole and wire process strip    │
     │ over a confidence-band                                                  │
     │ diagram plus a signals table. This is the densest slide in the deck,    │
     │ and the one that answers                                                │
     │ the track's actual bar.                                                 │
     │ - Top strip: Spoken event → Structured event → Deterministic posting →  │
     │ Three-layer match → Exception engine → Explained answer, six nodes on a │
     │ wire with punch-holes.                                                  │
     │ - Middle left: the confidence-band ladder. Three stacked ink-outlined   │
     │ bars showing                                                            │
     │ Exact ≥ 0.99 → auto-post, Fuzzy ≥ 0.90 → auto-post, 0.72 to 0.90 →      │
     │ model reasons, and is capped at 0.89 so it can never auto-post, < 0.72  │
     │ → exception. A bold in-chart callout sits                               │
     │ directly on the ladder at the 0.90 line: "The model cannot cross this   │
     │ line."                                                                  │
     │ - Middle right: the signals table, four rows (amount, date,             │
     │ counterparty name, reference) with                                      │
     │ their weights 0.40 / 0.20 / 0.25 / 0.15, plus the formula callout box   │
     │ beneath it, which                                                       │
     │ slide-archetypes.md says is not optional:                               │
     │ Confidence = 0.40·amount + 0.20·date + 0.25·name + 0.15·reference       │
     │ - Bottom band, rail WHAT WE MEASURE: a short table of the honest        │
     │ metrics, precision, recall,                                             │
     │ F1, exception count, and a one-line statement that a match rate alone   │
     │ can be gamed by matching                                                │
     │ nothing, so it is reported alongside precision and recall and never on  │
     │ its own.                                                                │
     │ - Source line: Track 04 brief | Method described in-deck.               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Slide 6 — Tech Stack. Archetype: layered architecture bands with a      │
     │ decides-vs-proposes                                                     │
     │ table.                                                                  │
     │ - Four stacked bands, each rail-labelled: Interface (voice in the       │
     │ merchant's own language,                                                │
     │ dashboard), Understanding (speech to structured event, explanation,     │
     │ question answering),                                                    │
     │ Truth (double-entry ledger, matching engine, tax rules, forecast        │
     │ model), Data                                                            │
     │ (voice-captured ledger, bank statement, settlement export, invoices).   │
     │ - Inside the Interface band, a small three-rung ladder graphic for      │
     │ graceful degradation:                                                   │
     │ full-duplex voice, then transcribe-only, then typed. The caption states │
     │ the principle in one                                                    │
     │ line: capability degrades, the ledger never stops accepting the event.  │
     │ Judges ask what happens                                                 │
     │ when the model is unavailable, and this answers it before the question  │
     │ is asked.                                                               │
     │ - A short language strip under the same band: the merchant speaks       │
     │ Hindi, Hinglish, Marathi,                                               │
     │ Tamil and more, and spoken Indian quantities (dhai, sava, derh, bara    │
     │ sau) are resolved                                                       │
     │ by deterministic code, not by the model. Claim only languages that have │
     │ actually been tested.                                                   │
     │ - The Understanding band is drawn in a visibly lighter treatment with a │
     │ dashed outline, and the                                                 │
     │ Truth band in solid primary with a heavy ink stroke. The visual states  │
     │ the argument before                                                     │
     │ anyone reads it.                                                        │
     │ - Right column: the decides-versus-proposes table, four rows. Money     │
     │ calculation, match posting,                                             │
     │ tax arithmetic, ledger balance: all deterministic, model never          │
     │ consulted. Speech understanding,                                        │
     │ ambiguous-candidate ranking, explanation phrasing: model proposes,      │
     │ engine bounds.                                                          │
     │ - Keep this vendor-light. Name capability categories rather than        │
     │ filenames or repos. No                                                  │
     │ screenshots, no links, nothing that reveals a built artefact.           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Slide 7 — Feasibility. Archetype: 3-horizon strip plus a cost-share     │
     │ segmented bar plus a                                                    │
     │ risk tree with 1:1 mitigations. Framed prospectively throughout.        │
     │ Headline: "Buildable in one villa night at ₹0 marginal cost, with the   │
     │ boundary stated."                                                       │
     │ - Horizon strip: Villa night (voice capture, the ledger, the            │
     │ three-layer matcher, the honest                                         │
     │ exception list), Next 30 days (forecast patterns, tax matching,         │
     │ WhatsApp settlement                                                     │
     │ reminders), Beyond (multi-shop, lender-facing credit view).             │
     │ - Cost-share segmented bar for the build cost, with the legend carrying │
     │ the values, not the                                                     │
     │ segments: model inference, hosting, database, all inside published free │
     │ tiers. Total marginal                                                   │
     │ cost near zero, which is itself the point.                              │
     │ - Risk tree, five risks each with a paired mitigation directly beneath  │
     │ in a calmer colour:                                                     │
     │ speech accuracy in a noisy shop → typed fallback path for every voice   │
     │ action; wrong match posted                                              │
     │ → the model cannot cross the auto-post line; model quota or             │
     │ availability fails mid-demo →                                           │
     │ the three-rung degradation ladder, capability drops but the ledger      │
     │ keeps accepting events;                                                 │
     │ no live UPI or Account Aggregator access for a non-regulated entity →   │
     │ simulate the rail and                                                   │
     │ state the boundary on this slide; data privacy under DPDP → consent and │
     │ purpose limitation                                                      │
     │ designed in, DPDP Rules notified 13 Nov 2025 with phased compliance to  │
     │ 13 May 2027.                                                            │
     │ - Explicit simulation-boundary box, gold: what is real, what is         │
     │ simulated, what would need a                                            │
     │ licence. Judges reward this. Never claim live UPI access.               │
     │ - Source line: RBI Digital Lending Directions 2025 | DPDP Rules, 13 Nov │
     │ 2025 | NPCI.                                                            │

     │ D4. Build mechanics                                                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Follow CLAUDE.md §6, in order, in a new Figma Slides file (the old file │
     │ is left alone):                                                         │
     │ 1. whoami for the planKey, then create_new_file with editorType:        │
     │ "slides". Do not reuse                                                  │
     │ or write into the previous deck's file.                                 │
     │ 2. listAvailableFontsAsync. Style strings are asymmetric: Inter uses    │
     │ "Semi Bold" with a                                                      │
     │ space, Poppins uses "ExtraBold" without one.                            │
     │ 3. Create all 9 slides empty in one script, capture the node ids.       │
     │ 4. Paste the figma-deck-kit.js preamble verbatim into every content     │
     │ script, with the retinted                                               │
     │ C block (§D2, unchanged from the original plan). Never re-derive        │
     │ addFrame or addText                                                     │
     │ inline, that is how the (−240, −240) position bug returns.              │
     │ 5. Build in batches of 2 to 3 slides. Append to the real parent before  │
     │ setting any position, at                                                │
     │ every level of nesting. Suggested batching: [Cover, Exec Summary,       │
     │ Problem], [Solution, How It                                             │
     │ Works], [Tech Stack, Feasibility], [Impact and Scope, Thank You].       │
     │ 6. Solution slide (4), specific fix for the defect found in the         │
     │ discarded build: reserve the                                            │
     │ headline's own horizontal band (roughly y=160 to y=230) and do not let  │
     │ any diagram node's                                                      │
     │ bounding box start above y=260. Draw the hub-and-spoke diagram with the │
     │ hub vertically centered                                                 │
     │ in the remaining space, and place the Voice capture node using the same │
     │ even-angle-spacing                                                      │
     │ formula as the other four spokes rather than hand-placing it "above"    │
     │ the hub — that hand                                                     │
     │ placement is what drifted into the headline zone last time. Confirm     │
     │ with the batch validator's                                              │
     │ textClip and outOfBounds checks, not by eye.                            │
     │ 7. Run the kit's batch validator after each batch. Screenshot after     │
     │ batch 1 before building the                                             │
     │ rest, and again after the Solution/How It Works batch given slide 4's   │
     │ history. Remember §7.8:                                                 │
     │ ribbon punch-holes and layered-card back-rects overlap by design, most  │
     │ validator flags on a                                                    │
     │ mature deck are these two patterns and not real bugs.                   │
     │ 8. cornerRadius = 0 sweep across the whole deck.                        │
     │ 9. Font-floor sweep, no content text under 22pt, source lines exempt by │
     │ name match.                                                             │
     │ 10. Screenshot every slide, check each against its archetype spec, fix, │
     │ re-sweep.                                                               │
     │ 11. Final read-through against the no-em-dash and no-slop rules,        │
     │ against the rule that no slide                                          │
     │ references a built application, and confirm the team block reads Harsh  │
     │ · Gursimran on                                                          │
     │ both the Cover and Thank You slides.                                    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ Execution order                                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Steps 1 and 2 are already complete, see A-state. Resume at step 3.      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 1. App scaffold, money, types, store layer. Done.                       │
     │ 2. Finance engine (posting, khata, tax, P&L) and the reconciliation     │
     │ core (normalize, signals,                                               │
     │ three-layer match, assign, exceptions, report, run). 60 tests passing.  │
     │ Done.                                                                   │
     │ 3. engine/forecast.ts and engine/inventory.ts, with their tests.        │
     │ 4. Seed generator with ground truth, then npm run seed, then            │
     │ eval/run.ts. Capture the real                                           │
     │ precision, recall and F1 here. Everything downstream quotes these       │
     │ numbers, so they have to                                                │
     │ exist before the README or any claim about accuracy gets written.       │
     │ 5. lib/owner.ts, the agent tool layer, and the text chat path. Text     │
     │ before voice, always: it is                                             │
     │ the bottom rung of the degradation ladder and has to be genuinely good. │
     │ 6. limits/budget.ts, then the Gemini Live layer and the voice dock.     │
     │ 7. The frontend: sidebar shell first, then the master dashboard (A9.2,  │
     │ the heavy one), then the                                                │
     │ three sub-dashboards. Load dataviz before the first chart and           │
     │ impeccable before the                                                   │
     │ dashboard grid.                                                         │
     │ 8. npm run verify, confirm the whole suite is green.                    │
     │ 9. README, using the real numbers from step 4. Done.                    │
     │ 10. GitHub, GCP and Firestore, Vercel, CI. Verify the live deployment.  │
     │ Done (GCP/Firestore                                                     │
     │ provisioning skipped for time, app runs on the file-store fallback; can │
     │ be added if asked).                                                     │
     │ 11. Deck content spec update — HISAAB-DECK-CONTENT.md revised per D1/D3 │
     │ above: Exec Summary                                                     │
     │ slide inserted, ribbon retagged to 7 labels, team block changed to      │
     │ Harsh · Gursimran, the                                                  │
     │ three density upgrades (issue-tree diagram, corrected hub-and-spoke,    │
     │ growth-roadmap strip)                                                   │
     │ written in. Self-reviewed against no-em-dash, no-slop, and              │
     │ no-built-app-reference rules.                                           │
     │ 12. Figma deck rebuild from scratch, new file, 9 slides, batched per    │
     │ D4, with the Solution-                                                  │
     │ slide layout fix applied deliberately (not re-discovered by trial and   │
     │ error), radius-zero and                                                 │
     │ font-floor sweeps, full screenshot review against each slide's          │
     │ archetype spec.                                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Step 4 gates every accuracy claim (already satisfied). Step 11 gates    │
     │ the Figma build: do not draw                                            │
     │ a node before the content spec update is settled, since fixing an       │
     │ argument in markdown costs                                              │
     │ minutes and fixing it in Figma costs an hour. Steps 1 to 10 are         │
     │ complete; resume at step 11.                                            │

     │ Verification                                                            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ What: Books balance                                                     │
     │ How: npm run test, the debits-equal-credits invariant asserted across   │
     │ the full                                                                │
     │ generated dataset                                                       │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ What: Reconciliation is honestly measured                               │
     │ How: npm run eval prints precision, recall and F1 against the           │
     │ generator's                                                             │
     │ ground truth, not a self-graded match rate                              │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ What: Known failure is reported, not hidden                             │
     │ How: The split-payment fixture asserts the engine fails and raises      │
     │ SPLIT_PAYMENT_SUSPECTED                                                 │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ What: The model cannot post money                                       │
     │ How: A test asserts every model-sourced confidence is clamped below the │
     │ 0.90                                                                    │
     │ auto-post threshold                                                     │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ What: Zero-setup claim is true                                          │
     │ How: Fresh clone, npm install && npm run dev, no env file, product      │
     │ works on the                                                            │
     │ file store                                                              │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ What: The master dashboard is genuinely dense                           │
     │ How: Screenshot / at 1440px and count: 8 KPI tiles and at least 12 data │
     │ panels                                                                  │
     │ present, every one carrying real engine-derived numbers, no placeholder │
     │ or                                                                      │
     │ filler panel, no dead space                                             │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ What: Navigation is four items, not nine                                │
     │ How: The sidebar carries Control, Reconcile, Khata, Books and nothing   │
     │ else;                                                                   │
     │ methodology opens as a sheet, khata detail is selection state rather    │
     │ than a                                                                  │
     │ route                                                                   │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ What: One click starts the agent talking                                │
     │ How: Click the sidebar voice button once, confirm the agent speaks      │
     │ first with a                                                            │
     │ grounded opener whose figures match what the engine reports for that    │
     │ day                                                                     │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ What: Voice path works                                                  │
     │ How: Live session records a multi-event spoken sentence, the tool trace │
     │ shows                                                                   │
     │ the events posted, and the Khata panel opens on screen while the agent  │
     │ speaks                                                                  │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ What: Merchant language works                                           │
     │ How: The same multi-event sentence spoken in Hinglish and in Hindi      │
     │ posts                                                                   │
     │ identical ledger entries, including dhai and derh quantities            │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ What: The ladder actually degrades                                      │
     │ How: Force the budget to zero and confirm the session opens on the next │
     │ rung,                                                                   │
     │ then on transcribe-only, then on typed, each with the right status chip │
     │ and                                                                     │
     │ no thrown error                                                         │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ What: The limiter defends the quota                                     │
     │ How: Fake-clock test proves the rolling window ceilings at 70% of TPM   │
     │ and the                                                                 │
     │ 8-minute cap closes the session with a working resume                   │

     │                                                                         │
     │ A1. Money and identity rules (write these first, everything depends on  │
     │ them)                                                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ src/lib/money.ts:                                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - All money is number in paise, integer only. Floats are banned in the  │
     │ domain layer.                                                           │
     │ A single rounding drift in a reconciliation demo is fatal on stage.     │
     │ - paise(rupees: number): number, formatINR(paise: number): string       │
     │ producing Indian                                                        │
     │ grouping (₹1,42,800.00), formatCompactINR for ₹1.43L / ₹84.3K on KPI    │
     │ tiles.                                                                  │
     │ - splitGst(totalPaise, ratePct) returns { basePaise, cgstPaise,         │
     │ sgstPaise } with the                                                    │
     │ remainder pushed into basePaise so the three always sum exactly to      │
     │ total.                                                                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Every entity id is nanoid(12) prefixed by type: txn_, ext_, mch_, exc_, │
     │ pty_, prd_.                                                             │
     │ Every transaction carries an immutable eventId so a re-run of           │
     │ reconciliation is idempotent.                                           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ A2. Domain model (src/lib/types.ts)                                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Firestore collections, all documents carrying ownerUid and scoped at    │
     │ the store layer the way                                                 │
     │ adhikaar/src/lib/store.ts does it (read that file before writing this   │
     │ one, the ownership-check                                                │
     │ pattern transfers directly).                                            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ type PartyKind = "customer" | "supplier";                               │
     │ type TxnType = "purchase" | "cash_sale" | "credit_sale" | "payment_in"  │
     │ | "payment_out" | "expense";                                            │
     │ type Source  = "voice" | "pos" | "bank" | "upi" | "invoice" | "manual"; │
     │ type Account = "CASH" | "BANK" | "RECEIVABLE" | "PAYABLE" | "SALES" |   │
     │ "PURCHASES"                                                             │
     │              | "GST_INPUT" | "GST_OUTPUT" | "INVENTORY";                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ interface Party      { id; ownerUid; kind: PartyKind; name;             │
     │ normalizedName; phone?; creditLimitPaise?; createdAt }                  │
     │ interface Product    { id; ownerUid; name; normalizedName; unit:        │
     │ "packet"|"kg"|"litre"|"piece";                                          │
     │                        unitPricePaise; gstRatePct: 0|5|12|18|28;        │
     │ stockQty }                                                              │
     │ interface TxnItem    { productId; productName; qty; unitPricePaise;     │
     │ lineTotalPaise; gstRatePct }                                            │
     │ interface Transaction{ id; ownerUid; eventId; date: string /* ISO date, │
     │ IST day */;                                                             │
     │                        type: TxnType; source: Source; partyId?;         │
     │ partyNameRaw?;                                                          │
     │                        amountPaise; taxPaise; reference?; items:        │
     │ TxnItem[];                                                              │
     │                        status: "open"|"settled"|"void"; note?;          │
     │ createdAt }                                                             │
     │ interface LedgerEntry{ id; ownerUid; transactionId; date; account:      │
     │ Account;                                                                │
     │                        debitPaise; creditPaise; memo }                  │
     │ interface ExternalRecord { id; ownerUid; source:                        │
     │ "bank"|"upi"|"invoice";                                                 │
     │                        date; narration; counterpartyRaw; amountPaise;   │
     │                        reference?; feePaise?; taxPaise?; direction:     │
     │ "credit"|"debit" }                                                      │
     │ interface MatchSignals { amountSim; dateSim; nameSim; refSim:           │
     │ number|null; daysApart; deltaPaise }                                    │
     │ interface Match      { id; ownerUid; internalTxnId; externalRecordId;   │
     │ confidence: number;                                                     │
     │                        signals: MatchSignals; decision:                 │
     │ "MATCHED"|"REVIEW"|"EXCEPTION";                                         │
     │                        method: "exact"|"fuzzy"|"ai"; reason: string;    │
     │ runId; createdAt }                                                      │
     │ interface Exception  { id; ownerUid; runId; kind: ExceptionKind;        │
     │ severity: "high"|"medium"|"low";                                        │
     │                        subjectIds: string[]; amountPaise?; explanation; │
     │ recommendedAction;                                                      │
     │                        status: "open"|"reviewed"|"resolved" }           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ type ExceptionKind =                                                    │
     │   | "UNMATCHED_LEDGER_ENTRY" | "UNMATCHED_BANK_CREDIT" |                │
     │ "UNMATCHED_BANK_DEBIT"                                                  │
     │   | "AMOUNT_MISMATCH" | "DUPLICATE_SUSPECTED" | "MISSING_INVOICE"       │
     │   | "UNKNOWN_COUNTERPARTY" | "GST_MISMATCH" | "SPLIT_PAYMENT_SUSPECTED" │
     │ | "DATE_OUT_OF_WINDOW";                                                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ A3. Store layer                                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Copy the exact router shape from adhikaar/src/lib/store.ts: one         │
     │ src/lib/store.ts that every                                             │
     │ route, action and page imports from, selecting between firestoreStore   │
     │ and fileStore on                                                        │
     │ whether FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID / FIREBASE_CLIENT_EMAIL /                   │
     │ FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY are present.                                       │
     │ fileStore writes to .data/hisaab.json locally and /tmp on Vercel        │
     │ (Adhikaar's                                                             │
     │ src/lib/store/fileStore.ts already solves the Vercel read-only-bundle   │
     │ problem, reuse that code                                                │
     │ almost verbatim).                                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Why this matters for the demo: the whole product runs with npm install  │
     │ && npm run dev and no                                                   │
     │ cloud account at all. That is a claim the README makes and a judge can  │
     │ verify in thirty seconds.                                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Also port adhikaar/src/lib/firestore/rateLimit.ts (30                   │
     │ writes/min/instance) unchanged. It is a                                 │
     │ free backstop against a scripted loop burning the free tier during a    │
     │ demo.                                                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ A4. The deterministic finance engine (src/lib/engine/)                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The single most important architectural rule in this product, and the   │
     │ one the deck argues:                                                    │
     │ the model never computes money. Every rupee is produced by the code     │
     │ below. The model                                                        │
     │ understands speech, chooses a tool, and reads back numbers the engine   │
     │ already computed.                                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ posting.ts — the double-entry poster. One exported function per event   │
     │ type, each returning                                                    │
     │ { transaction, ledgerEntries, effects }:                                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ┌──────────────────────────┬───────────────────────┬─────────────────── │
     │ ─┐                                                                      │
     │ │          Event           │         Debit         │       Credit       │
     │ │                                                                       │
     │ ├──────────────────────────┼───────────────────────┼─────────────────── │
     │ ─┤                                                                      │
     │ │ postPurchase (on credit) │ INVENTORY + GST_INPUT │ PAYABLE            │
     │ │                                                                       │
     │ ├──────────────────────────┼───────────────────────┼─────────────────── │
     │ ─┤                                                                      │
     │ │ postPurchase (cash)      │ INVENTORY + GST_INPUT │ CASH               │
     │ │                                                                       │
     │ ├──────────────────────────┼───────────────────────┼─────────────────── │
     │ ─┤                                                                      │
     │ │ postCashSale             │ CASH                  │ SALES + GST_OUTPUT │
     │ │                                                                       │
     │ ├──────────────────────────┼───────────────────────┼─────────────────── │
     │ ─┤                                                                      │
     │ │ postCreditSale           │ RECEIVABLE            │ SALES + GST_OUTPUT │
     │ │                                                                       │
     │ ├──────────────────────────┼───────────────────────┼─────────────────── │
     │ ─┤                                                                      │
     │ │ postPaymentIn            │ CASH or BANK          │ RECEIVABLE         │
     │ │                                                                       │
     │ ├──────────────────────────┼───────────────────────┼─────────────────── │
     │ ─┤                                                                      │
     │ │ postPaymentOut           │ PAYABLE               │ CASH or BANK       │
     │ │                                                                       │
     │ ├──────────────────────────┼───────────────────────┼─────────────────── │
     │ ─┤                                                                      │
     │ │ postExpense              │ PURCHASES             │ CASH or BANK       │
     │ │                                                                       │
     │ └──────────────────────────┴───────────────────────┴─────────────────── │
     │ ─┘                                                                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Every posting function asserts sum(debits) === sum(credits) and throws  │
     │ if not. A unit test                                                     │
     │ asserts the invariant across the entire generated dataset. That         │
     │ assertion is the honest basis                                           │
     │ for saying "the books balance", and it is a one-line answer to a judge  │
     │ asking how you know.                                                    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ inventory.ts — applies item quantities, flags negative stock as a soft  │
     │ warning (never blocks a                                                 │
     │ posting, because a real shopkeeper's count drifts and refusing the      │
     │ entry would lose the event).                                            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ khata.ts — per-party outstanding, aging buckets (0-7 / 8-15 / 16-30 /   │
     │ 30+ days), the                                                          │
     │ chronological item-level statement, and FIFO settlement of a payment    │
     │ against oldest open credit                                              │
     │ sales. settlePayment(partyId, amountPaise) returns which sales it       │
     │ consumed and the residual.                                              │
     │                                                                         │
     │ tax.ts — GST output from sales, GST input from purchases, net payable,  │
     │ and                                                                     │
     │ checkInvoiceTax(invoice) comparing the invoice's declared CGST+SGST     │
     │ against the rate-derived                                                │
     │ expectation, emitting a GST_MISMATCH exception when they differ by more │
     │ than ₹1.                                                                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ pnl.ts — revenue, COGS (weighted average cost), gross margin, expenses, │
     │ net, for a date range.                                                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ forecast.ts — the forward cash forecaster. Not an LLM, not Prophet. A   │
     │ transparent                                                             │
     │ recurrence model, which is a strength to state, not a limitation to     │
     │ hide:                                                                   │
     │ 1. Detect recurring supplier purchases: group by (partyId, productId),  │
     │ compute the median                                                      │
     │ inter-arrival gap in days and the median quantity. A pattern with 4+    │
     │ observations and a gap                                                  │
     │ standard deviation under 40% of the median becomes a RecurringPattern.  │
     │ 2. Project forward horizonDays by replaying each pattern at its median  │
     │ cadence.                                                                │
     │ 3. Project receivable collections using each party's own historical     │
     │ median days-to-pay, not a                                               │
     │ global assumption.                                                      │
     │ 4. Daily closing = opening + expected sales (trailing 14-day median     │
     │ daily sales) + expected                                                 │
     │ collections − projected purchases − scheduled payables − recurring      │
     │ expenses.                                                               │
     │ 5. Return { days: ForecastDay[], shortfallDate: string|null,            │
     │ shortfallPaise, drivers: {...} }                                        │
     │ where drivers names the three largest contributors, so the answer is    │
     │ explainable.                                                            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ This directly serves the user's stated example: a retailer restocks     │
     │ milk from the wholesaler on                                             │
     │ a rhythm, the forecaster learns the rhythm from the transaction history │
     │ rather than being told.                                                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ A5. Synthetic dataset generator (src/lib/seed/,                         │
     │ scripts/generate-dataset.ts)                                            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Seeded mulberry32 PRNG so the dataset is byte-identical on every run.   │
     │ Default seed 20260903.                                                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Shape: 60 days, 8 products, 14 customers, 5 suppliers, roughly 420      │
     │ internal transactions,                                                  │
     │ 390 bank statement lines, 150 UPI settlement rows, 60 supplier          │
     │ invoices. Well past the track's                                         │
     │ 50-record floor, and enough that a match rate means something.          │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Defect injection, deliberately documented in the app's Methodology page │
     │ and the README:                                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ┌─────────────────────────────┬───────┬──────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┐                                                                    │
     │ │           Defect            │ Count │           What it tests         │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├─────────────────────────────┼───────┼──────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ Clean exact match           │ 300   │ Baseline throughput             │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├─────────────────────────────┼───────┼──────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ Counterparty name variation │ 40    │ Fuzzy name matching             │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ │  only                       │       │                                 │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├─────────────────────────────┼───────┼──────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ Bank posting lag, 1 to 4    │ 25    │ Date tolerance                  │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ │ days                        │       │                                 │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├─────────────────────────────┼───────┼──────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ Settlement fee delta, ₹2 to │ 20    │ AMOUNT_MISMATCH detection       │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ │  ₹40 short                  │       │                                 │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├─────────────────────────────┼───────┼──────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ Duplicate record            │ 12    │ DUPLICATE_SUSPECTED             │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├─────────────────────────────┼───────┼──────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ Missing counterpart in bank │ 10    │ UNMATCHED_LEDGER_ENTRY          │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├─────────────────────────────┼───────┼──────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ Unknown counterparty        │ 8     │ UNKNOWN_COUNTERPARTY            │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ │ narration                   │       │                                 │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├─────────────────────────────┼───────┼──────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ GST declared wrong on       │ 6     │ Tax-line matcher                │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ │ invoice                     │       │                                 │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├─────────────────────────────┼───────┼──────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ Split payment, one sale     │ 5     │ Expected to fail. Reported as a │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ │ settled by two credits      │       │ known limitation.               │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ └─────────────────────────────┴───────┴──────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┘                                                                    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The generator writes a groundTruth: Array<{internalTxnId,               │
     │ externalRecordId}> file alongside the                                   │
     │ data. This is the thing that separates this submission from every other │
     │ team's. With ground                                                     │
     │ truth you can report precision, recall and F1, not just a self-graded   │
     │ "match rate". A match rate                                              │
     │ alone can be gamed by matching nothing and calling everything an        │
     │ exception. Say that out loud in                                         │
     │ the deck and the README.                                                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ A6. Reconciliation engine (src/lib/recon/)                              │
     │                                                                         │
     │ normalize.ts                                                            │
     │ - Dates to IST calendar day, yyyy-MM-dd.                                │
     │ - Names: uppercase, strip PVT, LTD, LIMITED, PRIVATE, ENTERPRISES,      │
     │ TRADERS, & SONS,                                                        │
     │ strip UPI narration prefixes (UPI/CR/, UPI/DR/, IMPS/, NEFT/, POS/),    │
     │ strip digits                                                            │
     │ when they are a reference block, collapse whitespace.                   │
     │ - References: uppercase alphanumeric only.                              │
     │                                                                         │
     │ signals.ts — four pure, individually testable functions returning 0 to  │
     │ 1:                                                                      │
     │ - amountSim(a, b) = a === b ? 1 : max(0, 1 - |a-b| / max(a,b))          │
     │ - dateSim(a, b) = max(0, 1 - daysApart / 7)                             │
     │ - nameSim(a, b) — token-set similarity: split both into tokens, for     │
     │ each token in the shorter                                               │
     │ set take the best Levenshtein ratio against the longer set, average.    │
     │ Implement Levenshtein                                                   │
     │ in-repo (about 20 lines), no dependency.                                │
     │ - refSim(a, b) — longest common substring length divided by the longer  │
     │ reference length.                                                       │
     │ Returns null when either reference is absent.                           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ match.ts — the three layers.                                            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Layer 1, exact. Same normalized reference AND identical amount.         │
     │ Confidence 0.99,                                                        │
     │ method exact. No model involved, no fuzzy math.                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Layer 2, weighted fuzzy. Blocking first, or the run is O(n²) and slow   │
     │ on stage: bucket                                                        │
     │ candidates by amount rounded to the nearest ₹1 and by ±5 day window,    │
     │ union the two candidate                                                 │
     │ sets. Then score:                                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ score = 0.40*amountSim + 0.20*dateSim + 0.25*nameSim + 0.15*refSim      │
     │ When refSim is null, drop that term and rescale the other three to sum  │
     │ to 1. Bands:                                                            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ┌──────────────┬─────────────────────────┐                              │
     │ │     Band     │        Decision         │                              │
     │ ├──────────────┼─────────────────────────┤                              │
     │ │ >= 0.90      │ MATCHED, method fuzzy   │                              │
     │ ├──────────────┼─────────────────────────┤                              │
     │ │ 0.72 to 0.90 │ REVIEW, sent to Layer 3 │                              │
     │ ├──────────────┼─────────────────────────┤                              │
     │ │ < 0.72       │ Not a candidate         │                              │
     │ └──────────────┴─────────────────────────┘                              │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Layer 3, model reasoning, tightly caged. Only REVIEW-band pairs go to   │
     │ Gemini, batched, with                                                   │
     │ structured output validated by a zod schema. The prompt hands the model │
     │ only the candidates the                                                 │
     │ deterministic layer already produced and asks it to pick one or none,   │
     │ with reasoning.                                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Three hard constraints, all enforced in code, not in the prompt:        │
     │ 1. The model may only return an id that was in the candidate list.      │
     │ Anything else is discarded.                                             │
     │ 2. The model may not return an amount. The engine keeps its own.        │
     │ 3. The model's confidence is clamped to a maximum of 0.89, below the    │
     │ auto-post threshold.                                                    │
     │ Therefore a model decision can never auto-post as MATCHED. It always    │
     │ lands as REVIEW with                                                    │
     │ its explanation attached, for a human to accept.                        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ That third constraint is the deck's second contrarian beat and the      │
     │ honest answer to "the 2026                                              │
     │ bottleneck is verification capacity, not generation speed". Everyone    │
     │ else will make the LLM the                                              │
     │ engine. Hisaab makes it the interface and the explainer, and caps its   │
     │ authority in code.                                                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ assign.ts — one-to-one greedy assignment over scored pairs, descending  │
     │ by confidence, marking                                                  │
     │ each record consumed. State plainly in the README that this is greedy   │
     │ and not optimal                                                         │
     │ (Hungarian), and that greedy is chosen because it is explainable per    │
     │ pair, which matters more                                                │
     │ here than the last fraction of a percent.                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ exceptions.ts — everything unmatched, plus matched-but-delta pairs,     │
     │ becomes a typed                                                         │
     │ Exception with a written explanation and recommendedAction. Never       │
     │ silently force a                                                        │
     │ match. Every exception is generated by deterministic code so the list   │
     │ is reproducible.                                                        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ report.ts — computes and stores per run:                                │
     │ totalInternal, totalExternal, matchedCount, reviewCount,                │
     │ exceptionCount, matchRatePct, precision, recall, f1,                    │
     │ totalVariancePaise, runtimeMs, precision and recall measured against    │
     │ the                                                                     │
     │ generator's ground truth.                                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ A7. The agent layer (src/lib/agent/)                                    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ tools.ts — eight function declarations shared by both the Live voice    │
     │ session and the text                                                    │
     │ chat fallback. Each has a zod schema for its arguments and a handler    │
     │ that calls the engine.                                                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Tool: record_business_events                                            │
     │ Purpose: The big one. Accepts an array of typed events                  │
     │ (inventory_purchase,                                                    │
