     │ Live model once. Adhikaar hit exactly this class of failure with        │
     │ gemini-2.5-flash, which is                                              │
     │ why the chain exists at all.                                            │
     │ - Dialog chain in order: resolved Gemini 3 Flash Live id, then          │
     │ gemini-2.5-flash-native-audio-preview-12-2025, then transcribe-only     │
     │ degradation (A8.2).                                                     │
     │ Build the same generateWithFallback-style chain Adhikaar uses in        │
     │ src/lib/gemini/client.ts,                                               │
     │ moving to the next entry on any request-level failure, empty response,  │
     │ or 429.                                                                 │
     │ - Ephemeral tokens, so the standing API key never reaches the browser.  │
     │ Server side:                                                            │
     │ const client = new GoogleGenAI({});                                     │
     │ const token = await client.authTokens.create({                          │
     │   config: {                                                             │
     │     uses: 1,                                                            │
     │     expireTime: new Date(Date.now() + 30*60*1000).toISOString(),        │
     │     newSessionExpireTime: new Date(Date.now() + 60*1000).toISOString(), │
     │   },                                                                    │
     │ });                                                                     │
     │ - Ephemeral tokens work only against the Live API and only on v1beta.   │
     │ Client connects with                                                    │
     │ new GoogleGenAI({ apiKey: token.name }) then ai.live.connect({ model,   │
     │ config, callbacks }).                                                   │
     │ - Audio in: raw 16-bit PCM, little endian, mono, 16 kHz, mime           │
     │ audio/pcm;rate=16000.                                                   │
     │ Audio out: raw 16-bit PCM at 24 kHz (different rate, use two            │
     │ AudioContexts).                                                         │
     │ - Config: responseModalities: [Modality.AUDIO],                         │
     │ inputAudioTranscription: {},                                            │
     │ outputAudioTranscription: {}, systemInstruction, tools: [{              │
     │ functionDeclarations }].                                                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ public/worklets/pcm-recorder.js — an AudioWorkletProcessor that         │
     │ converts Float32 frames to                                              │
     │ Int16 and posts them to the main thread in ~100 ms chunks. Do not use   │
     │ ScriptProcessorNode.                                                    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ src/lib/gemini/live.ts + src/components/voice/useLiveSession.ts handle: │
     │ - Mic capture via getUserMedia({ audio: { channelCount: 1,              │
     │ echoCancellation: true } }).                                            │
     │ - A playback queue on a 24 kHz AudioContext, buffers scheduled back to  │
     │ back. On an                                                             │
     │ interrupted server message, flush the queue immediately so barge-in     │
     │ feels instant.                                                          │
     │ - toolCall messages: POST each call to /api/agent/tool, then reply with │
     │ sendToolResponse.                                                       │
     │ Tool execution stays server side, always. The browser never touches the │
     │ ledger.                                                                 │
     │ - Session resumption handles, so a dropped connection reconnects        │
     │ without losing context.                                                 │
     │ - Every tool call is rendered live in a ToolTrace strip under the       │
     │ transcript. A judge watching                                            │
     │ the demo sees record_business_events → 3 events posted → ₹1,240         │
     │ receivable created. Showing                                             │
     │ the mechanism is worth more than hiding it.                             │
     │                                                                         │
     │ A8.2 Merchant-language mode (not an English product with translation    │
     │ bolted on)                                                              │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The user's plan carries all four Live models with unlimited RPM and     │
     │ RPD, so multilingual is a                                               │
     │ designed capability here, not a stretch goal. It is also the honest     │
     │ requirement: the person this                                            │
     │ product is for does not narrate their day in English.                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - Default posture: speak whatever the merchant speaks. Native-audio     │
     │ dialog models handle                                                    │
     │ language selection natively and can switch mid-conversation without a   │
     │ separate translation                                                    │
     │ step. Do not force a language picker as a gate. Offer one as an         │
     │ override only.                                                          │
     │ - Hinglish is the expected default, not an edge case. "Do packet doodh  │
     │ aaye Sharma Traders se,                                                 │
     │ Rekha ne teen chips udhaar liye." The system instruction states this    │
     │ explicitly and gives                                                    │
     │ worked examples, because a model told only "support Hindi" will         │
     │ over-correct into formal Hindi                                          │
     │ that no shopkeeper speaks.                                              │
     │ - Numbers are the failure point, so they are handled in code, not by    │
     │ the model. Indian                                                       │
     │ spoken quantities (dhai = 2.5, sava = 1.25, paune = 0.75, derh = 1.5,   │
     │ bara sau =                                                              │
     │ 1200, lakh, crore) get a deterministic normalizer in                    │
     │ src/lib/agent/numerals.ts applied to                                    │
     │ tool arguments before posting, with its own test fixtures. A model that │
     │ mishears dhai kilo as                                                   │
     │ 2 kilo writes a wrong ledger entry, and no amount of prompt engineering │
     │ makes that safe.                                                        │
     │ - gemini-3.5-live-translate-preview earns its place on a real use case, │
     │ not as a demo                                                           │
     │ garnish: a Tamil-speaking retailer and a Hindi-speaking wholesaler      │
     │ settling a supply account.                                              │
     │ Each hears their own language, both sides post into one ledger. Wire it │
     │ as a distinct                                                           │
     │ "counterparty call" mode, separate from the single-merchant dialog      │
     │ mode.                                                                   │
     │ - Verify the actual demo language before the pitch. The research doc in │
     │ this folder already                                                     │
     │ flags that platform-wide language counts and native-audio voice         │
     │ coverage are reported                                                   │
     │ differently by different sources. Test the exact languages the demo     │
     │ will use, in the room, and                                              │
     │ never assert a language count on stage that has not been tried.         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ A8.3 The rate limiter                                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The user asked for one explicitly. Because RPM and RPD are unlimited    │
     │ and only TPM binds, this is                                             │
     │ a token budget limiter with a degradation ladder, not a request         │
     │ counter. Ported from and                                                │
     │ sitting alongside Adhikaar's src/lib/firestore/rateLimit.ts pattern.    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ src/lib/limits/budget.ts:                                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - A per-model rolling 60-second token window, ceilinged at 70% of the   │
     │ account's real TPM                                                      │
     │ (105K of 150K for Gemini 3 Flash Live, 700K of 1M for the 2.5           │
     │ native-audio fallback, 70K of                                           │
     │ 100K for transcribe and translate). The 30% headroom absorbs the        │
     │ estimation error that comes                                             │
     │ from countTokens being unavailable on Live models.                      │
     │ - Accounting per active session: 25 tokens/second for streamed input    │
     │ audio, the same constant                                                │
     │ for streamed output, plus the system instruction and tool declarations  │
     │ charged once at session                                                 │
     │ open, plus a growing session-memory term, since memory re-bills at      │
     │ input rate. Reconcile                                                   │
     │ the estimate against usageMetadata whenever the server sends it, and    │
     │ let the real figure                                                     │
     │ overwrite the estimate.                                                 │
     │ - Session duration caps, which is where the real protection lives: soft │
     │ warning to the user                                                     │
     │ at 6 minutes, hard close at 8, with a one-click resume that opens a     │
     │ fresh session carrying a                                                │
     │ short deterministic summary of the ledger state rather than the full    │
     │ transcript. Enable the                                                  │
     │ Live config's context-window compression so the re-billed memory stays  │
     │ bounded inside a                                                        │
     │ session too.                                                            │
     │ - Per-user guards: one active Live session per user, a minimum 2-second │
     │ gap between token mints                                                 │
     │ to stop a retry loop, and a hard daily ceiling per user that exists     │
     │ purely as a bug backstop.                                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ /api/voice/token consults the budget before minting. If the budget is   │
     │ exhausted it does not                                                   │
     │ error, it degrades, in this order:                                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 1. Primary dialog model → 2.                                            │
     │ gemini-2.5-flash-native-audio-preview-12-2025, which carries the        │
     │ 1M TPM headroom → 3. transcribe-only mode: gemini-3.5-transcribe-live   │
     │ streams speech to                                                       │
     │ text, and the existing text agent does the reasoning and tool calling,  │
     │ so the merchant still                                                   │
     │ talks and the ledger still posts, only the reply arrives as text        │
     │ instead of speech →                                                     │
     │   d. typed input, the text chat path, which never depended on any of    │
     │ this.                                                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Each rung is visible in the UI as a small honest status chip, voice ·   │
     │ full duplex,                                                            │
     │ voice · transcribe only, typed, rather than a silent quality drop. The  │
     │ product never dies                                                      │
     │ on stage, and the ladder itself is a defensible engineering answer to a │
     │ judge asking what                                                       │
     │ happens when the quota runs out.                                        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Two more limiters, both cheap:                                          │
     │ - Sliding-window per user on /api/agent/tool and /api/chat, guarding    │
     │ the text path.                                                          │
     │ - The ported Firestore write limiter at 30 writes per minute per        │
     │ instance, guarding the free                                             │
     │ tier against a scripted loop.                                           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Test all three with fake clocks: budget exhaustion triggers the exact   │
     │ next rung, the duration cap                                             │
     │ closes the session and the resume carries state, and the write limiter  │
     │ refuses the 31st write.                                                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ A8.4 Text fallback                                                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Text fallback is mandatory. /api/chat runs generateContent with the     │
     │ identical tool                                                          │
     │ declarations, driving a normal chat panel. If the venue wifi or the     │
     │ room noise kills the voice                                              │
     │ demo, the whole product still demos. Build this before the voice path,  │
     │ not after. It is also the                                               │
     │ bottom rung of the degradation ladder above, so it has to be genuinely  │
     │ good, not a stub.                                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ A9. Frontend: one dense master dashboard, three sub-dashboards, one     │
     │ voice button                                                            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ A9.0 The shape, decided                                                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Four nav destinations total. Not nine routes. One master dashboard      │
     │ carrying extreme content                                                │
     │ density, then exactly three sub-dashboards for the jobs that need their │
     │ own working surface,                                                    │
     │ switched from buttons down the left sidebar. One voice button lives in  │
     │ that same sidebar, above                                                │
     │ Settings, and is reachable from every screen.                           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ┌───────────┬────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ────┐                                                                   │
     │ │ Nav item  │   Route    │                       Job                    │
     │    │                                                                    │
     │ ├───────────┼────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ────┤                                                                   │
     │ │ Control   │ /          │ The master dashboard. Everything, at once,   │
     │    │                                                                    │
     │ │           │            │ dense                                        │
     │    │                                                                    │
     │ ├───────────┼────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ────┤                                                                   │
     │ │ Reconcile │ /reconcile │ The matching workbench and the exception     │
     │ list   │                                                                │
     │ ├───────────┼────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ────┤                                                                   │
     │ │ Khata     │ /khata     │ Party credit, master and detail on one       │
     │ screen   │                                                              │
     │ ├───────────┼────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ────┤                                                                   │
     │ │ Books     │ /books     │ Forecast, GST and P&L                        │
     │    │                                                                    │
     │ └───────────┴────────────┴───────────────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ────┘                                                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Three deliberate collapses from the earlier draft, each because a       │
     │ separate route was not earning                                          │
     │ its place: the old /exceptions folds into /reconcile as a tab (an       │
     │ exception is a                                                          │
     │ reconciliation outcome, and splitting them made the reconcile screen    │
     │ look thinner than it is),                                               │
     │ /khata/[id] becomes selection state inside /khata rather than a         │
     │ navigation, and /forecast                                               │
     │ plus /tax merge into /books since both answer "what do the books say    │
     │ about money that has                                                    │
     │ not moved yet". /methodology stops being a route and becomes a          │
     │ slide-over sheet from the                                               │
     │ sidebar footer, so it stays available to a judge without occupying a    │
     │ nav slot.                                                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ There is no marketing landing page. / is the dashboard. The deck        │
     │ carries the argument; the app                                           │
     │ carries the product.                                                    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ A9.1 Design language                                                    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Dense, data-grade, ink on paper. Not a purple SaaS gradient. Echoes the │
     │ deck without copying it.                                                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - Ground #FAFAF8, ink #0A0A0A, indigo primary #14306E, ledger green     │
     │ #1E7A5A for matched                                                     │
     │ and positive, rupee gold #E8B33C for review and watch, exception red    │
     │ #D8453C.                                                                │
     │ - Corner radius 2px maximum, echoing the deck's radius-0 doctrine. 1px  │
     │ ink hairlines on every                                                  │
     │ panel and table cell rather than shadows. Panels sit edge to edge in a  │
     │ grid with a 1px gutter,                                                 │
     │ so the screen reads as one instrument rather than a scatter of floating │
     │ cards.                                                                  │
     │ - Every figure in tabular-nums. JetBrains Mono for amounts and ids,     │
     │ Inter for body,                                                         │
     │ a display face for headings. Columns of rupee figures that do not align │
     │ vertically are the                                                      │
     │ single fastest way to look amateur in a finance product.                │
     │ - Charts via shadcn's <ChartContainer> (Recharts underneath, already    │
     │ installed).                                                             │
     │ Load the dataviz skill before writing the first chart, and the          │
     │ impeccable skill before                                                 │
     │ laying out the master dashboard grid. Density is the goal, clutter is   │
     │ the failure mode, and                                                   │
     │ those two skills are what keep them apart.                              │
     │                                                                         │
     │ A9.2 The master dashboard, / (the screen that has to land)              │
     │                                                                         │
     │ This is the one the user asked to be genuinely heavy: a technical judge │
     │ should look at it and                                                   │
     │ register immediately that there is a real system underneath. Target     │
     │ roughly 8 KPI tiles and                                                 │
     │ 12 to 14 distinct data panels on one screen, in a 12-column grid, zero  │
     │ dead space, no                                                          │
     │ decorative filler. Every number traces to the deterministic engine      │
     │ already built.                                                          │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Row 1, the KPI strip. Eight tiles, each with a label, a large tabular   │
     │ numeral, a delta chip                                                   │
     │ against the prior period, and a micro-sparkline:                        │
     │ Cash position · Bank balance · Receivables (udhaar out) · Payables ·    │
     │ Match rate · Open                                                       │
     │ exceptions · Net GST payable · 30-day net profit.                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Row 2. Cash position line chart with the forecast continuing as a       │
     │ dashed extension and a                                                  │
     │ marked shortfall point (6 cols) · reconciliation donut, Matched /       │
     │ Review / Exception, with the                                            │
     │ total in the centre and a precision / recall / F1 micro-table beside it │
     │ (3 cols) · live exception                                               │
     │ feed, severity dot, amount, kind chip (3 cols).                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Row 3. Multi-source ingestion bars, one stacked bar per source (voice   │
     │ ledger, bank, UPI,                                                      │
     │ invoices) split into matched / review / exception, which is the Side A  │
     │ versus Side B idea from                                                 │
     │ the reference screenshots compressed into a summary (5 cols) · khata    │
     │ aging stacked bar across                                                │
     │ the four buckets plus the top five debtors (4 cols) · today's activity  │
     │ feed of voice-captured                                                  │
     │ events (3 cols).                                                        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Row 4. Inflow versus outflow grouped bars across 14 days with a running │
     │ balance line                                                            │
     │ (5 cols) · GST input versus output with net payable and the             │
     │ tax-exception count (4 cols) ·                                          │
     │ detected recurring patterns, each with its cadence and next expected    │
     │ date, which is the                                                      │
     │ forecaster showing its working (3 cols).                                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Row 5. Recent matches table, full width, last twelve rows: transaction, │
     │ counterparty, amount,                                                   │
     │ source, an inline confidence bar, decision chip, method chip. Row click │
     │ opens the same audit                                                    │
     │ drawer /reconcile uses, so the component is written once.               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ A9.3 The three sub-dashboards                                           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ /reconcile. A four-tile mini KPI strip (processed, match rate, total    │
     │ variance, open                                                          │
     │ exceptions), then the split Side A / Side B tables with a segmented     │
     │ status bar over each, exactly                                           │
     │ the reference-screenshot pattern. Tabs: All · Matched · Review ·        │
     │ Exceptions · Manually matched.                                          │
     │ Row click opens the audit drawer: confidence ring, the four signal bars │
     │ (amount, date, name,                                                    │
     │ reference) with their weights shown, the delta in rupees, the written   │
     │ reason, the recommended                                                 │
     │ action, and an Accept / Reject pair. On an exception, a "Why didn't you │
     │ match this?" button                                                     │
     │ asks the agent and streams the explanation into the drawer, grounded in │
     │ explain_match.                                                          │
     │                                                                         │
     │ /khata. Master and detail on one screen, no navigation between them.    │
     │ Left: You'll get /                                                      │
     │ You'll give / Net balance tiles, search, and the party list with an     │
     │ aging chip per row. Right:                                              │
     │ the selected party's full statement, item level, showing quantity, unit │
     │ price, GST, date and a                                                  │
     │ running balance, plus the aging bar, that party's own median            │
     │ days-to-pay, and payment history.                                       │
     │ A "Send reminder" control is present but clearly marked as not wired,   │
     │ since WhatsApp automation                                               │
     │ is a stated Phase 2 item and pretending otherwise would be the exact    │
     │ dishonesty this project                                                 │
     │ argues against.                                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ /books. Three stacked sections. Forecast: projected daily balance line  │
     │ over inflow and                                                         │
     │ outflow bars, a shortfall banner naming the date and the amount, the    │
     │ named drivers, and the                                                  │
     │ recurring-pattern table. Tax: GST input versus output, net payable, and │
     │ the tax exception table                                                 │
     │ with expected versus recorded and the delta. P&L: revenue, COGS, gross  │
     │ margin, expenses, net,                                                  │
     │ with a period selector.                                                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ A9.4 The sidebar and the one voice button                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Fixed left rail, icon-only at narrow widths, expanding to about 240px.  │
     │ Top to bottom:                                                          │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 1. Brand mark: Hisaab with हिसाब beneath it.                              │
     │ 2. The four nav buttons from A9.0, active state carried by a 2px indigo │
     │ left edge.                                                              │
     │ 3. Spacer.                                                              │
     │ 4. The voice button. Visually the loudest control in the app: filled    │
     │ indigo, larger than the                                                 │
     │ nav items, with a mic glyph. One click starts listening. Not            │
     │ press-and-hold, not a                                                   │
     │ keyboard chord as the primary path (⌘K stays as a secondary shortcut).  │
     │ Click again, or say                                                     │
     │ so, to stop.                                                            │
     │ 5. A live status chip directly beneath it: voice · full duplex, voice · │
     │ transcribe only, or                                                     │
     │ typed, driven by the degradation ladder in A8.3 so the current          │
     │ capability is never a                                                   │
     │ mystery.                                                                │
     │ 6. Settings.                                                            │
     │ 7. Methodology, which opens the honesty sheet.                          │
     │ 8. An account slot showing Prototype · no sign-in, so a judge is not    │
     │ left wondering whether                                                  │
     │ they missed a login.                                                    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The voice experience itself (this is the differentiator, so it gets     │
     │ built carefully):                                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - One click opens the Live session and the agent speaks first, with     │
     │ real context. On session                                                │
     │ open the client sends a deterministic primer assembled by engine code,  │
     │ not by the model:                                                       │
     │ today's transaction count, the credit-sale count, cash position, and    │
     │ open exception count. So                                                │
     │ the opener is grounded, something like "Aaj barah sales hui hain, do    │
     │ udhaar par. Cash position                                               │
     │ chaurasi hazaar hai. Kya record karna hai?" It never invents an opening │
     │ figure because it is                                                    │
     │ reading numbers the engine handed it.                                   │
     │ - A voice dock slides in from the right while the session is live,      │
     │ carrying: a listening-state                                             │
     │ waveform, the live input transcript, the agent's reply transcript, and  │
     │ the tool trace strip                                                    │
     │ where each tool call appears as a chip with its result summary. Showing │
     │ the mechanism is worth                                                  │
     │ more than hiding it, and it is what makes a judge believe the ledger    │
     │ actually moved.                                                         │
     │ - Barge-in works: speaking over the agent flushes the playback queue    │
     │ immediately.                                                            │
     │ - focus_dashboard fires alongside spoken answers, so asking about       │
     │ Rekha's udhaar opens /khata                                             │
     │ with Rekha selected while the agent is still talking. Voice and screen  │
     │ are one interaction.                                                    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ A9.5 Authentication, deliberately deferred                              │
     │                                                                         │
     │ No login for the prototype. A single DEMO_OWNER_UID constant in         │
     │ src/lib/owner.ts is the                                                 │
     │ owner for every store call. This costs nothing later because the store  │
     │ layer already takes                                                     │
     │ ownerUid on every read and write and checks it internally, so real auth │
     │ is a change to one                                                      │
     │ function rather than to every call site. That seam is already built and │
     │ tested. Say so in the                                                   │
     │ README's "what is not built" section rather than leaving it as an       │
     │ unexplained gap.                                                        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ A10. Tests and evaluation                                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Vitest, targeting roughly 90 to 120 real tests. Not coverage theatre,   │
     │ these specific things:                                                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - money.test.ts — Indian grouping, GST split summing exactly to total,  │
     │ no float leakage.                                                       │
     │ - posting.test.ts — every event type debits equal credits; the          │
     │ invariant holds across the                                              │
     │ entire generated dataset.                                               │
     │ - signals.test.ts — each of the four signal functions, fixtured pass    │
     │ and fail.                                                               │
     │ - match.test.ts — a fixture per injected defect class, asserting the    │
     │ decision the engine should                                              │
     │ reach. The split-payment fixture asserts it fails, and asserts it       │
     │ produces a                                                              │
     │ SPLIT_PAYMENT_SUSPECTED exception rather than a wrong match.            │
     │ - assign.test.ts — no external record is ever claimed twice.            │
     │ - khata.test.ts — FIFO settlement, aging bucket boundaries at exactly   │
     │ 7, 15 and 30 days.                                                      │
     │ - forecast.test.ts — a synthetic weekly restock pattern is detected     │
     │ with the right cadence.                                                 │
     │ - tax.test.ts — a ₹50 GST mismatch is caught, a ₹0.50 rounding          │
     │ difference is not.                                                      │
     │ - agent/tools.test.ts — every tool handler against a fixed store,       │
     │ asserting spokenSummary                                                 │
     │ contains the engine's number and the handler rejects malformed args.    │
     │ - agent/numerals.test.ts — dhai, sava, paune, derh, bara sau, lakh,     │
     │ crore and                                                               │
     │ their Devanagari forms resolve to the right quantity, and an            │
     │ unrecognised token is rejected                                          │
     │ rather than guessed.                                                    │
     │ - limits/budget.test.ts — with a fake clock: the rolling window         │
     │ ceilings at 70% of TPM, budget                                          │
     │ exhaustion selects the exact next rung of the ladder, the 8-minute cap  │
     │ closes the session, the                                                 │
     │ resume carries deterministic ledger state, and the Firestore limiter    │
     │ refuses the 31st write.                                                 │
     │ - gemini/client.test.ts — the model fallback chain, mocked at the SDK   │
     │ boundary (port                                                          │
     │ Adhikaar's version of this test).                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ eval/run.ts — runs the full reconciliation against the ground truth and │
     │ prints a table:                                                         │
     │ records processed, matched, review, exceptions, precision, recall, F1,  │
     │ mean confidence on                                                      │
     │ correct matches vs incorrect ones, and runtime. This output is what the │
     │ README's evaluation table                                               │
     │ quotes, and it is the honest version of the track's "measured accuracy" │
     │ requirement.                                                            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ PART B — Deployment                                                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ All CLI. gh is already authenticated as harshkawatra11. vercel@54.13.0  │
     │ and gcloud are                                                          │
     │ installed. Firebase tooling runs via npx firebase-tools.                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 1. git init, .gitignore covering .env*.local, .data/, .next/,           │
     │ service-account JSON.                                                   │
     │ 2. Confirm identity before the first commit:                            │
     │ git config user.name "Harsh Kawatra" and git config user.email          │
     │ "harshkawatra11@gmail.com".                                             │
     │ 3. gh repo create harshkawatra11/hisaab --public --source=.             │
     │ --remote=origin --push.                                                 │
     │ Public, so a judge can open it.                                         │
     │ 4. GCP and Firestore:                                                   │
     │ gcloud projects create hisaab-app --name="Hisaab"                       │
     │ gcloud config set project hisaab-app                                    │
     │ gcloud services enable firestore.googleapis.com                         │
     │ gcloud firestore databases create --location=asia-south1                │
     │ gcloud iam service-accounts create hisaab-store                         │
     │ gcloud projects add-iam-policy-binding hisaab-app \                     │
     │   --member="serviceAccount:hisaab-store@hisaab-app.iam.gserviceaccount. │
     │ com" \                                                                  │
     │   --role="roles/datastore.user"                                         │
     │ gcloud iam service-accounts keys create ./sa.json \                     │
     │   --iam-account=hisaab-store@hisaab-app.iam.gserviceaccount.com         │
     │ 4. Scope the service account to roles/datastore.user only, exactly as   │
     │ Adhikaar does, then                                                     │
     │ delete sa.json from disk once the values are in Vercel. Set a GCP       │
     │ billing budget alert at                                                 │
     │ ₹1 as the second safety layer.                                          │
     │ 5. vercel link, then vercel env add for GEMINI_API_KEY,                 │
     │ FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID,                                                    │
     │ FIREBASE_CLIENT_EMAIL, FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY, SESSION_SECRET, across     │
     │ production,                                                             │
     │ preview and development.                                                │
     │ 6. .github/workflows/ci.yml: lint, then typecheck, then test, then next │
     │ build, cheapest                                                         │
     │ first. Do not add a standalone tsc --noEmit step ahead of the build if  │
     │ it proves flaky                                                         │
     │ on a clean checkout, Adhikaar hit exactly that failure because Next     │
     │ generates ambient route                                                 │
     │ types as a build side effect.                                           │
     │ 7. vercel --prod. Verify the live URL, then verify Firestore            │
     │ persistence across serverless                                           │
     │ instances with an independent curl against a different instance, the    │
     │ way the Adhikaar README                                                 │
     │ documents. That verification is a README claim, so it has to actually   │
     │ be run.                                                                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Commit discipline: small, real commits with plain messages, no em       │
     │ dashes, and the Co-Authored-By                                          │
     │ trailer. Never force-push main.                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ PART C — The README                                                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Model it directly on adhikaar/README.md, which is attached to this task │
     │ as the reference. Same                                                  │
     │ sections, same order, Hisaab's content:                                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 1. Centered header: name, one-line positioning, shields for live app,   │
     │ tests, license, Next.js,                                                │
     │ TypeScript, Firestore, Gemini, then an anchor nav row.                  │
     │ 2. "The claim this is built on" — the sourced problem statement. The    │
     │ MSME credit gap of                                                      │
     │ ₹20 to 25 lakh crore (U.K. Sinha RBI Expert Committee, June 2019); PM   │
     │ SVANidhi's 1.12 crore                                                   │
     │ loans worth ₹17,800 crore to 75.5 lakh street vendors (MoHUA); 89%      │
     │ account ownership with 16%                                              │
     │ of accounts inactive (Global Findex 2025). Then the honest caveat, the  │
     │ way Adhikaar caveats                                                    │
     │ its own register: these establish the size of the informal-credit       │
     │ economy, they are not a                                                 │
     │ measurement of reconciliation error rates, and no public                │
     │ transaction-level Indian vendor                                         │
     │ dataset exists, which is why the data here is synthetic and the         │
     │ generator is documented.                                                │
     │ 3. "One loop, not four separate tools" — the four track directions as   │
     │ consequences of one                                                     │
     │ ledger. Make the point structurally, not just in prose: the app is one  │
     │ master dashboard plus                                                   │
     │ three working surfaces, because reconciliation, settlement Q&A,         │
     │ forecasting and tax are four                                            │
     │ views of one ledger rather than four products bolted together. A        │
     │ screenshot of the master                                                │
     │ dashboard carries this argument faster than a paragraph does.           │
     │ 4. Mermaid mindmap of the actual stack, grouped by what each library    │
     │ does in this codebase.                                                  │
     │ 5. Technology cards table — every row tied to a real file path.         │
     │ 6. Mermaid architecture graph — voice and model layers drawn as a       │
     │ clearly separate,                                                       │
     │ optional, dashed side branch feeding only understanding and             │
     │ explanation, never computation.                                         │
     │ This mirrors Adhikaar's anti-wrapper doctrine drawing and is the visual │
     │ thesis of the repo.                                                     │
     │ 7. Mermaid sequence diagram — the reconciliation moneyshot: spoken      │
     │ event, structured event,                                                │
     │ posting, bank record arrives, three-layer match, exception raised,      │
     │ agent explains.                                                         │
     │ 8. Testing and evaluation — the real test count and the real            │
     │ precision/recall/F1 table from                                          │
     │ npm run eval. Publish the failures too, including the split-payment     │
     │ class, exactly as                                                       │
     │ Adhikaar publishes its 56.4% classifier number. A system that only      │
     │ reports its successes is                                                │
     │ not trustworthy, and this one is asked to make money-adjacent           │
     │ decisions.                                                              │
     │ 9. Running locally, zero-setup claim, then the optional Gemini and      │
     │ Firestore sections.                                                     │
     │ 10. Deployment, CI/CD (both halves verified against a real failure, not │
     │ assumed).                                                               │
     │ 11. "What this system decides, what it merely proposes" table. The rule │
     │ stated plainly:                                                         │
     │ the model may listen, structure and explain. It may never compute or    │
     │ post. Add a short                                                       │
     │ subsection on the token-budget limiter and the three-rung degradation   │
     │ ladder, with the real                                                   │
     │ TPM ceilings and the reasoning that session length, not session count,  │
     │ is what burns a Live                                                    │
     │ API quota. That paragraph is the kind of detail that reads as           │
     │ operational maturity.                                                   │
     │ 12. What is covered and what is not: the simulation boundary. No live   │
     │ UPI rails, no NPCI                                                      │
     │ membership, no Account Aggregator, no bank core write access. Synthetic │
     │ data, documented                                                        │
     │ generator. Not an accounting system of record and not tax advice. State │
     │ the deferred                                                            │
     │ login plainly here rather than leaving a judge to discover it: the      │
     │ prototype runs as a                                                     │
     │ single demo merchant, and the reason it costs nothing to add real auth  │
     │ later is that the                                                       │
     │ store layer already takes and checks ownerUid on every read and write,  │
     │ so authentication                                                       │
     │ slots into one function instead of every call site. WhatsApp settlement │
     │ reminders are named                                                     │
     │ as Phase 2 and the button in the Khata screen says so on its face.      │
     │ 13. Apache 2.0.                                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Same writing rules as everything else: no em dashes, no hooks, no slop. │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ PART E — README-only enrichment: name the backend-depth gap as roadmap, │
     │ don't build it (NEW)                                                    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Context, revised                                                        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Comparing fintech/README.md against adhikaar/README.md side by side:    │
     │ the two are already                                                     │
     │ structurally close (same mermaid mindmap / architecture graph /         │
     │ sequence diagram pattern,                                               │
     │ technology cards, a decides-vs-proposes table, an honest evaluation     │
     │ section). What's thinner is                                             │
     │ the backend itself — Adhikaar has more distinct engineered subsystems   │
     │ (a jurisdiction                                                         │
     │ classifier, an 18-rule legality linter, a cron-driven deadline sweep,   │
     │ real PDF generation, real                                               │
     │ auth) than Hisaab currently does.                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Given a real deadline on other work, this gap is closed in the README   │
     │ only, as an honestly                                                    │
     │ labelled roadmap, not by writing new engine code. No new .ts files, no  │
     │ new tests, no new                                                       │
     │ tools, no new UI this pass. This mirrors the exact move the current     │
     │ README already makes for                                                │
     │ WhatsApp reminders ("named as Phase 2... the button says so on its      │
     │ face") — extend that same                                               │
     │ honest-roadmap treatment to a couple more named future subsystems,      │
     │ rather than fabricating                                                 │
     │ capability that doesn't exist. The PPT (Part D) is the active priority  │
     │ right now; this part is                                                 │
     │ lower priority and should only be picked up once Part D is done, or     │
     │ skipped entirely if time                                                │
     │ runs out — it does not block or gate the deck in either direction.      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ E1. What to add to the README, as roadmap, not as done                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Under "What is covered, and what is not" (or a new short "What's next"  │
     │ subsection right after                                                  │
     │ it), name two concrete, specific future subsystems, the same level of   │
     │ specificity Adhikaar uses                                               │
     │ for things it also hasn't built, so the roadmap reads as engineered     │
     │ rather than aspirational:                                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - Credit-risk scoring. One sentence naming the actual signals a real    │
     │ version would use (aging                                                │
     │ profile, the party's own median days-to-pay, reconciliation exception   │
     │ load, tenure), and the                                                  │
     │ actor it serves: a moneylender pricing risk on the ledger's own history │
     │ instead of guessing.                                                    │
     │ State plainly that this is not built: no creditScore.ts exists yet.     │
     │ - Deterministic settlement reminders. One sentence naming what the      │
     │ "Send reminder" button in                                               │
     │ /khata would generate if wired: a templated message with the            │
     │ outstanding amount and the                                              │
     │ oldest unpaid item's age, composed by deterministic string templating   │
     │ the same way                                                            │
     │ spokenSummary is elsewhere in the app, never by the model. State        │
     │ plainly that today the button                                           │
     │ is present but inert, exactly as the current README already says.       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Do not add these to the mermaid mindmap, the architecture graph, the    │
     │ technology cards table,                                                 │
     │ or the "what this system decides" table — those describe what exists. A │
     │ roadmap item belongs only                                               │
     │ in prose, under a clearly labelled "not built yet" section, so the      │
     │ diagrams stay honest.                                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ E2. Same treatment in the deck (Part D below)                           │

     │ and the README agree with each other and neither overclaims. This costs │
     │ nothing extra in the                                                    │
     │ Figma build since that slide and its horizon strip are already speced   │
     │ in D3 below; it is a content                                            │
     │ substitution, not a new element.                                        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Execution note                                                          │
     │                                                                         │

     │ PART D — The HackerVilla deck (the defining deliverable) — REVISED      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Context for this revision                                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The app, deployment and README (Parts A–C) are done and verified. The   │
     │ deck (Part D) was                                                       │
     │ started once already: Cover, Problem and Solution slides were built in  │
     │ a Figma Slides file, but                                                │
     │ on review the Solution slide had a real construction defect (the "Voice │
     │ capture" card, placed                                                   │
     │ above the hub, visually overlapped and obscured the slide headline —    │
     │ "…ssing side first, then                                                │
     │ reconcile it" was cut off behind the yellow card) and the hub-and-spoke │
     │ diagram read sparse                                                     │
     │ rather than EY-dense. The user has explicitly asked to discard that     │
     │ build and start the deck                                                │
     │ completely from scratch in a new Figma file, after re-grounding in the  │
     │ inspiration decks                                                       │
     │ (ppts-inspo/, 13 files confirmed on disk: EY, Goal Diggers, BCG,        │
     │ Binance, Asian Paints, ITC,                                             │
     │ Nestle, Samsung, Salesforce, Schneider, Nilkamal, Foreteach,            │
     │ Star_Inspire) and the                                                   │
     │ case-competition-deck skill's structure and rhetoric patterns, so the   │
     │ density and construction                                                │
     │ discipline are stronger the second time. Two decisions locked with the  │
     │ user for this revision:                                                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - New Figma Slides file. The old file is left untouched as a reference  │
     │ for what to avoid.                                                      │
     │ - 9 slides, not 8. A dedicated Executive Summary slide is inserted at   │
     │ position 2, directly                                                    │
     │ after Cover — the skill's corpus treats this as the single              │
     │ highest-leverage slide in a winning                                     │
     │ deck (recommendation locked in one sentence, two-column for/against),   │
     │ and the existing content                                                │
     │ spec didn't have one as a standalone slide.                             │
     │ - Team name is Harsh and Gursimran only, everywhere a team block        │
     │ appears (Cover, Thank You).                                             │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Everything else already decided in this plan stands: the contrarian     │
     │ thesis, the retinted palette                                            │
     │ (§D2 below, unchanged), the "never disclose the app" rule, zero em      │
     │ dashes, and India-first sourced                                         │
     │ figures. This section replaces the old Part D in full — Parts A, B and  │
     │ C above are unaffected and                                              │
     │ already complete.                                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Built natively in Figma Slides via use_figma, 1920×1080, following      │
     │ CLAUDE.md §1 to §8 and                                                  │
     │ deck-system/. Content-engineered first with the case-competition-deck   │
     │ skill, then rendered.                                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ D0. The density bar, measured (new, from a direct study of the          │
     │ references)                                                             │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Rendered ppts-inspo/EY.pdf and Goal Diggers_DTU.pdf to PNG and read     │
     │ them, and inspected six                                                 │
     │ Figma Slides reference decks directly. Node count per slide is the      │
     │ objective density metric,                                               │
     │ and it is the single clearest diagnosis of why the discarded build fell │
     │ short:                                                                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ┌──────────────────┬────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────┐ │
     │ │  Reference deck  │        File key        │ Nodes per content slide │ │
     │ ├──────────────────┼────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────┤ │

     │                                                                         │
     │ Target for every Hisaab content slide (3 through 8): 90 to 160 nodes.   │
     │ Under 80 means a                                                        │
     │ content block is missing, not that the slide is "clean". Cover and      │
     │ Thank You are exempt and stay                                           │
     │ deliberately sparse.                                                    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ What the EY pages actually do (studied directly, pages 3, 4 and 6):     │
     │ - Four to six distinct visual forms per slide, never two. EY p4 alone   │
     │ runs an arc funnel, a                                                   │
     │ stacked bar chart, a convergence diagram, three giant-numeral columns,  │
     │ a horizontal bar set, and                                               │
     │ a branching goals tree, all on one page.                                │
     │ - The equation layout is a real structural device: [form A] + [form B]  │
     │ = [form C] with                                                         │
     │ literal large + and = glyphs between content blocks.                    │
     │ - Tables carry a formula callout directly beneath them in a tinted box  │
     │ (EY p6:                                                                 │
     │ Overall Viability = Demand × Scalability × Regulatory Support ×         │
     │ Innovation ÷ Capital Intensive).                                        │
     │ A scoring table without its formula shown reads as arbitrary.           │
     │ - Paired contrast panels: Learnings (green tint) beside Obstacles (red  │
     │ tint), same shape, same                                                 │
     │ bullet count. Also Market GAPS as a green callout fed by a dashed red   │
     │ annotation arrow drawn from                                             │
     │ the "Weaknesses" row of the table above it.                             │
     │ - Vertical dashed dividers split a slide into left/right halves;        │
     │ horizontal dashed dividers                                              │
     │ split it into bands. Both are load-bearing structure, not decoration.   │
     │ - A rotated rail label on the left edge of a band ("Rationale", "GTM    │
     │ Strategy").                                                             │
     │                                                                         │
     │ What the ConsentGrid house style does (this is the direct template to   │
     │ match, since it is this                                                 │
     │ folder's own prior deck at the target standard):                        │
     │ - 8-tag ribbon with punch-holes and connecting wires; active tag =      │
     │ primary fill, white text,                                               │
     │ layered back-card. Section chip directly beneath it.                    │
     │ - Band + rail is the primary layout skeleton: a thin colored vertical   │
     │ bar on the left, a small                                                │
     │ colored label pill at its head (MARKET SIZE, REGULATORY CLOCK, PROOF,   │
     │ READINESS & COST),                                                      │
     │ and a dashed divider running right from the pill across the slide. Two  │
     │ to three such bands stack                                               │
     │ per slide. Build every content slide on this skeleton first, then fill  │
     │ the bands.                                                              │
     │ - Inside the bands: nested TAM/SAM/SOM circles, CAGR bar sets with      │
     │ value pills, an alternating                                             │
     │ above/below timeline with colored dot nodes, layered giant-numeral stat │
     │ cards, a TRL ladder of                                                  │
     │ color-coded progress bars with black badge chips, and risk rows as ⚠    │
     │ risk → mitigation pairs on                                              │
     │ alternating gray bands.                                                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ One correction to inherit deliberately: ConsentGrid's headlines use em  │
     │ dashes ("This is not                                                    │
     │ speculative — privacy-preserving collaborative AI already beats siloed  │
     │ AI"). The Hisaab deck must                                              │
     │ not copy that. Match the structure and density, not the punctuation.    │
     │ Use a comma, a colon, or                                                │
     │ two sentences.                                                          │
     │                                                                         │
     │ D1. Before drawing a single node                                        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 1. Re-ground in the reference material already read during this         │

     │ This is a compressed                                                    │
     │ 9-slide deck (not the ~100-slide GCCH scale), so only the sections that │
     │ survive compression                                                     │
